# S2-11 failure and unknown attribution

This report attributes the immutable S2-10 canonical aggregate. It does not replace, modify, or convert any original outcome. A shard-level replay command for every row, including the frozen snapshot and SQLite evidence identifiers, is in the companion JSON ledger.

- Source aggregate: [`outputs/acceptance/phase2-s210-census-interim-20261012.json`](phase2-s210-census-interim-20261012.json)
- Aggregate report SHA-256: `bef7b141d791a49eafa844b9f792afcb967511ef507a080520d163ab6069f6f2`
- Aggregate file SHA-256: `b8841f28979783b873e7ffb3ff5eb3b748a69b575d85bd4a292424c5176273ba`
- Frozen snapshot: `p2-41649744e057d61031d5`; units: 11,380; formal shards: 512/512.
- Attributed rows: 2056 (1,235 failed + 821 unknown). Unit outcomes remain 0 passed, 1,235 failed, 9,324 skipped, 821 unknown.
- SQLite stage jobs: 30,843 passed, 1,235 failed, 35,380 skipped, 822 unknown; these 68,280 job rows are not the 11,380-unit denominator.
- Native-passed / federated-failed pairs: 0. Federated and compare are skipped for missing fixture/loader coverage, so this empty list does not establish parity.
- All per-unit records include exact stage result hashes, job/result hashes, attempt IDs, run/shard identity, artifact/profile identity, and a deterministic command to rerun the entire original shard using a separate SQLite DB.

## Verified report-suppression artifacts

The cache bytes were rehashed against the canonical artifact identities and the exact packed source member was inspected.

| Package | Artifact SHA-256 | Archive member | Source | Verified cache path |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `koishi-plugin-boom` | `357b171f4fb9c6460f3ba29497779919232741e17279ce725cb00746d9fa645e` | `package/lib/index.js` | `process.exit(0);` | `Ligamina-artifact-cache/sha256/ab/aba87728d52d6e8938eee17dd6a5f7131272b4cfdb0d6eba9882f74dc24552b0a5902b7a4032aa3038b144242a71c559ba1f4b170acc7178d45049e4eb70df43` |
| `koishi-plugin-boom2` | `045a3208eed955661fc7a518f863930f5737e8b8b89f3a7fcf46429b0b9dd06f` | `package/index.js` | `process.exit(0)` | `Ligamina-artifact-cache/sha256/8e/8e6ab855b5131858bb729778e0774a358dff396f0b1f56272d58bc2ca2852fe87bf6db386cd5dad24e67fa7770ef864efae37bb8bc3a3a00f39a6fc665fdf5cd` |

## Interpretation boundaries

- `failed` records an observed failed stage; the outcome is not automatically a Ligamina defect. Native-load cases include package/profile/API mismatches and missing imports; offline npm installation cases include measured Node heap exhaustion and other locked install failures.
- `unknown` remains unknown for App Control Win32 4551, preparation errors, timeouts and unavailable Worker backends. Do not bypass host policy or count these as pass/fail compatibility evidence.
- Four report-invalid rows resolve to two frozen Koishi packages whose source calls `process.exit(0)` (`koishi-plugin-boom` and `koishi-plugin-boom2`) and consequently suppresses the probe report. This is an evidence-capture defect: S2-11 will make that termination a structured native-load failure, then rerun scoped regressions and the complete frozen census.
- No `native_success/federated_failure` pair is present because federated/compare never ran under missing entry-bound fixture or loader conditions. The outcome must remain an explicit coverage gap.
- This ledger does not claim a compatibility pass. Behavior remains not evaluated; real-platform validation remains not run.
